# Strategy

**Document date:** 2026-07-28 (revision 43); amended 2026-08-18 (Rev 45); amended 2026-08-24 (Rev 46); amended 2026-08-25 (Rev 47); amended 2026-09-05 (Rev 48)

**Rev 48 changelog (2026-09-05, owner directive) — THE SHOCK / OVERLAY AXIS GETS A WRITTEN GRADING RUBRIC WITH A REQUIRED DE-ESCALATION RULE; THE RE-RISKING LIMB'S DWELL CLOCK IS CORRECTED FROM THE LATEST ROW TO THE UNBROKEN RUN; AND THREE FROZEN REGIME-AXIS TOKEN SETS ARE ALIGNED TO THE ENUMS M1a ACTUALLY WRITES.** Provenance: the owner's in-session instruction, this session, 2026-09-05, to implement the reviewed recommendation package. Three items, all in this document; none changes any strategy's Entry criteria, Exit rules, thresholds, indicators, sizing rules or kill criteria, and none moves any `spec_locked_since` or `immutable_since`. **(a) `## Regime scoring (strategy-blind, monthly)` gains `### Shock / overlay grading rubric`.** The axis had three tokens and, as `ops.alerts` `shock_overlay_override_unbounded` recorded on 2026-09-03 out of the August divergence-review cohort, no stated de-escalation criterion anywhere — a scoring could raise it on judgment with nothing saying when to lower it. The rubric defines a qualifying shock event against input category 4's existing inclusion criteria, a pre-shock baseline (60-trading-day median of Brent front-month closes from `state.signal_marks_curated`, `ticker = 'BZUSD'` — or the median of the full available history where fewer than 60 sessions precede the first qualifying event, which is the limb in force today at 43 observations) and a shock elevation, states entry conditions for all three grades, and makes the `acute` → `latent` downgrade REQUIRED once both of its legs hold (zero new qualifying events for 15 consecutive trading days AND Brent retraced at least 50% of the shock elevation). Escalation stays immediate on any qualifying event with active transmission; the asymmetry is deliberate and is now stated rather than implied. Every grading must record the most recent qualifying event's date, the baseline / peak / current Brent triplet, and which legs held. The rubric CONSTRAINS judgment rather than replacing it — a deviating scoring must argue against these anchors in the rationale — and it was calibrated against the axis's own recorded history (2026-07-01 `latent`, 2026-08-01 `acute`, 2026-09-01 `acute`), which it reproduces. It does NOT discharge the same alert's other two limbs, horizon scaling and a per-strategy transmission term, which remain referred to their owning surface. **(b) The Rev 46 re-risking limb's condition (a) is corrected.** Rev 46 specified the 15-trading-day DO-NOT-ACTIVATE dwell as trading days elapsed since the LATEST `STRATEGY_ACTIVATION` row's `as_of_date`, which meant any no-change re-affirmation — routine monthly and adversarial-review restatements both — reset the clock to zero. Measured 2026-09-05: the literal reading returned 1 trading day for every strategy on the roster, against true unbroken-run lengths of 22 trading days for two of them, so the symmetric re-risking path Rev 46 added had been unreachable in practice for most of its life. The dwell now counts `state.market_calendar` trading days from the START of the current unbroken DO-NOT-ACTIVATE run, with re-affirmations EXTENDING the run rather than resetting it, computed for every active strategy by `state.rerisking_limb_status`. The limb's other two legs, its conjunctive structure, and its REFRESH-never-a-flip character are unchanged, byte for byte; item (a) above also gives leg (b) the dated, measured anchor it was written to require. **(c) Three axis token sets in `## Regime scoring (strategy-blind, monthly)` are aligned to the operative enums** — `disinflationary` → `disinflating`, `complacent / normal` → `risk-on / neutral`, `contained` → `latent` — closing the drift `ops.alerts` `regime_axis_vocabulary_drift` has carried since M1b raised it. The alignment moves the FROZEN text to the tokens M1a has actually been writing for four months, rather than the reverse, which would have made that history non-comparable on three axes. `acute` and `stressed` are deliberately UNTOUCHED: both are reconciliation-rule keys, and `acute` additionally drives a live capital path. Stating it plainly so it is not mistaken for fixed: this alignment does NOT repair the `risk_sentiment = stressed` reconciliation rule's fail-open — `stressed` has never once been emitted, and aligning the vocabulary around it changes nothing about that; it remains open. `spec_hash` is recomputed for all five strategies (three shared hash inputs moved: `strategy/00_preamble.md` for this changelog, `strategy/09_regime_scoring_strategy_blind_monthly.md` for items (a) and (c), and the shared `### Pre-mortem: Regime router` segment of `strategy/08_pre_mortems.md` for item (b)); `spec_locked_since` is NOT moved for any of them.

**Rev 47 changelog (2026-08-25, owner directive) — STRATEGY E ENTRY CRITERION 3's "≥ 95TH PERCENTILE" QUANTITATIVE DIVERGENCE ANCHOR IS DROPPED AS A GATE; SPREAD-PERCENTILE MEASUREMENT AND LOGGING ARE DELIBERATELY RETAINED.** Rev 46 (below, item (a)) flagged this requirement as UNRATIFIED BUT CURRENTLY BINDING and referred its drop-or-ratify disposition to the owner, naming five dependents a drop would require restating in the same revision: the Pair-prioritization rule, the 2.4 central-risk mitigation, Constraint 2's flowing/universe/residual clauses, Constraint 3 component (a)'s 2.24 mitigation, and Known Limitation 14. **The owner has now decided: drop it.** The anchor existed only in the E pre-mortem's prose (introduced rev 2, tightened to 95th at rev 3 per cycle 2 T2.B, via SL2's documentary-only route (B), which is explicitly forbidden from altering entry criteria) — never in this document's canonical `## Strategy E` → Entry criteria and never in `strategy_math/strategy_e.py`, which has only ever defined `MIN_PAIR_CORRELATION_ENTRY = 0.5`, `CORRELATION_BREAKDOWN_THRESHOLD = 0.3`, `TIME_EXIT_DAYS = 182`, and `MAX_FINANCING_COST_PCT_OF_EXPECTED_RETURN = 0.15` — yet it was enforced as a hard NO-GO gate from rev 2 forward, including as one of three independent grounds for the 2026-08-18 TLN/VST decline alongside entry criterion 5's Anchor 2 (dropped the same day, Rev 45 companion). **What is dropped:** the anchor's power to BLOCK an entry. The E pre-mortem's entry criterion 3 reverts to exactly what canonical `Strategy.md` and `strategy_math/strategy_e.py` have always specified — 252-day L-S correlation ≥ 0.5, beta-adjusted leg sizing — with no percentile floor on admission. **What is deliberately RETAINED, because three Section 6 logging fields and one live monitoring indicator depend on it being measured:** the spread is still COMPUTED (classified price-ratio-vs-log-spread by the existing 3x-share-price-ratio rule) and still RECORDED at entry as `spread_definition`, `reference_window`, and `percentile_at_entry` — the last of which now spans its true 0.00–1.00 range, since entries are no longer confined to the top 5% — and the entry-percentile-vs-realized-P&L rank correlation indicator, which survives and in fact gains the ability to actually test its hypothesis now that `percentile_at_entry` will vary. The Pair-prioritization rule survives, restated to rank candidates by measured percentile rather than to gate on it. Known Limitation 14 (the 3x share-price-ratio parameter) survives, since the spread-definition rule it documents still governs a live measurement. **Honest cost, stated plainly, not papered over:** E's residual risk on 2.4 (narrative over-fit), 2.24 (cross-session inconsistency), **and 2.17 (algorithm appreciation bias on the 0.50 correlation discontinuity) RISES** — the anchor's exclusionary force, cited at all three loci as either external-to-reasoning verification or a second mechanism-enforced threshold, is gone, leaving only the recorded observation (2.17's residual is the sharpest of the three: with the anchor gone, the 0.50 correlation discontinuity is E's only remaining mechanism-enforced entry threshold) — and the owner accepted that trade in making this directive. This removes an accretion that was never legitimately adopted into canonical criteria or code, not a weakening of a ratified control: the pair-trading literature the anchor cited (Gatev/Goetzmann/Rouwenhorst 2006; Avellaneda/Lee 2010) was never the defect, and canonical `## Strategy E` → Entry criteria and `strategy_math/strategy_e.py` are **unchanged** by this revision because the anchor was never in them — this is a reversion to the ratified spec, not a new loosening of it. **Sixth dependent, found during this revision's own verification sweep, beyond the five Rev 46 named:** Section 5's 2.17 disadvantage entry and Known Limitation 12 also cited the anchor, in current tense, as a live "second mechanism-enforced threshold" partially offsetting 2.17's bright-line-deference risk — Rev 46's flag did not catch this locus, and it is restated here alongside the other five rather than left standing. Full restatement of every affected locus (entry criterion 3, the Pair-prioritization rule, the 2.4 central-risk mitigation, Constraint 2's flowing/universe/residual clauses, Constraint 3 component (a)'s 2.24 mitigation, Known Limitation 14, Section 5's 2.17 entry, Known Limitation 12, and Section 6's logging fields) is in the E pre-mortem, now rev 13 — see `### Pre-mortem: Strategy E` below. This revision does **not** adjudicate, fold in, or close the open `premortem-E-2026-a3` AR_orc adversarial cycle (live SL2 queue item `revise-premortem-E-2026-a3`), which continues independently. `spec_hash` is recomputed for Strategy E (the E pre-mortem segment moves; canonical `## Strategy E` and `strategy_math/strategy_e.py` do not); `spec_locked_since` is NOT moved.

**Rev 46 changelog (2026-08-24) — STRATEGY E PRE-MORTEM PROVENANCE FLAG + EXIT-RULE MIRROR; A SYMMETRIC RE-RISKING LIMB ON THE ROUTER'S OUT-OF-CYCLE REFRESH RULE; STRATEGY C DOCUMENTARY FIXES (CYCLE 15); AND AN UNSOURCED-FIGURE PROVENANCE SWEEP ACROSS ALL FIVE STRATEGY PRE-MORTEMS.** None of the five items below is an owner directive, and none is a machinery change to any strategy's canonical Entry/Exit criteria or `strategy_math/`: **(a) Entry criterion 3's "≥ 95th percentile" quantitative divergence anchor is FLAGGED, not dropped.** A review of the E pre-mortem (`strategy/08_pre_mortems.md`) found this requirement exists only in that pre-mortem's prose — never in canonical Strategy E's Entry criteria and never in `strategy_math/strategy_e.py` — introduced via SL2's documentary-only route (B) at rev 2/rev 3, yet enforced as a hard NO-GO gate ever since, including as one of three grounds for the 2026-08-18 TLN/VST decline alongside entry criterion 5's Anchor 2 (which the owner dropped that same day, `Strategy.md` Rev 45 companion). Unlike Anchor 2, this anchor is load-bearing elsewhere in the pre-mortem (the Pair-prioritization rule's trigger, the 2.4 central-risk mitigation, Constraint 2's flowing/universe/residual clauses, Constraint 3's component (a) 2.24 mitigation, and Known Limitation 14 all cite it), so dropping it would be a substantive risk change requiring those five dependents to be restated in the same revision — not a call this revision makes silently. Instead, the pre-mortem's entry-criterion-3 locus gets a bracketed provenance/status annotation recording the requirement's origin, its UNRATIFIED-BUT-CURRENTLY-BINDING status (corroborated by `Annual_Constraint_Audit.md` C-E-13, already carrying it as unresolved), and the two legitimate resolutions — ratify into canonical criteria + code, or drop with all five dependents restated — both reserved to the owner. **Nothing about E's live behavior changes; the anchor continues to bind exactly as it does today.** **(b) The Rev 45 short-leg-stop's Exit-rules mirror and `exit_reason` logging gap are closed** (AR_orc `premortem-E-2026-a3` cycle 11, item T1-2 — closing the deferral rev 11 recorded 2026-08-18). The E pre-mortem's Section 1 Exit rules gains a fifth bullet mirroring canonical `Strategy.md`'s mandatory short-leg stop, and Section 6's `exit_reason` enum gains a matching fifth value, `short_leg_stop` — a stop-triggered exit previously had no legal value to log. **(c) The regime router's out-of-cycle fundamental-refresh rule (Section 6, misclassification scenario 3) gains a symmetric re-risking limb.** Every existing out-of-cycle trigger (scenarios 1 and 3) is a deterioration trigger; D2's 2026-08-13 router review recorded that asymmetry explicitly — five-for-five unmet, three unmet in the risk-REDUCING direction — leaving a strategy gated OFF mid-month unable to be un-gated before the next monthly M1a cycle. An out-of-cycle refresh is now ALSO permitted and required when a strategy has held DO-NOT-ACTIVATE for ≥15 consecutive trading days (measured mechanically from `events.regime_events` against `state.market_calendar`), the specific reconciliation-override axis responsible has a dated, measured reversal in its own primary inputs, and the technical plane is simultaneously benign (VIX ≠ HIGH, SPY Trend ≠ DOWN, Breadth = HEALTHY) — deliberately conjunctive where the de-risking triggers are disjunctive, and a REFRESH (re-running M1a/M1b, reconciled and adjudicated as normal) rather than a flip; it grants no daily routine new authority to score a fundamental axis itself. **(d) Strategy C's pre-mortem closes four AR_orc `premortem-C-2026-a3` cycle 15 findings (documentary; the open cycle itself is NOT adjudicated or closed by this revision).** T1-1: a `[At 15 closed trades]` Section 6 checklist bullet is added — the "15-trade gate" was cited at least six times elsewhere in C's section but unreachable from the monthly checklist a session actually executes; the new bullet consolidates the cumulative readings Known Limitations 11 and 13 already specify, inventing no new indicator or threshold. T1-2: the Code-stack health check bullet's owner is reassigned from "the participant" to **M4**, mirroring KL 13's own rev-14 reassignment and its justification (mirrored from Strategy E's "Size-attack transmission audit" bullet) — rev 13 had declined the identical reassignment as "machinery, not documentation" while rev 14 performed it for KL 13 in the same breath; the operative check is unchanged, only the named owner moves. T1-3 (retiered Tier 2): Known Limitation 3 was mischaracterized as "triggerless" in rev 14's own sweep note despite being counted among the seven KLs that DO carry a trigger and despite its own text literally carrying one — corrected to state the real, narrower defect (the trigger it carries states no magnitude or consequence verb). T1-4: see (e) below. **(e) The unsourced "~30%/~70%" narrative-over-fit figure is flagged at its primary locus in ALL FIVE strategy pre-mortems (A, B, C, D, E), not only the two AR_orc had independently flagged (Strategy C cycle 15 T1-4; Strategy E cycle 11 T1-1).** The figure traces to `AI_Trading_Foundation.md` §1.3's unattributed sentence, uncited since the repository's first commit (`e7be4c0`, 2026-05-08); A1's 2026-07-28 annual re-derivation searched for a source at all four evidence levels with two independent agents and recorded it ABSENT (`Annual_AI_Foundation_Sweep.md` §C.3), remedy NEW RESEARCH rather than a replication on the current model line — `AI_Trading_Foundation.md` §§1.3 and 2.4 both already carry that flag verbatim. A bracketed provenance annotation is added at each strategy's primary 2.4/Constraint-2 locus (A and B's Section 5 2.4 entries; C's Constraint 2 flowing limitation, since its Section 5 entry omits the figure; D's Section 5 2.4 entry; E's Section 5 2.4 entry) stating the figure is unsourced, that 2.4's EXISTENCE claim and narrative-over-fit MECHANISM are unaffected (only the MAGNITUDE is unsupported), and that it should be read as an unsourced working assumption rather than a measurement; the figures themselves are NOT removed, since they remain load-bearing for each section's own residual arithmetic. Strategy A's pre-existing honest framing for its own size-attack instance of the figure (rev 9/10) is extended to, not contradicted by, the new Section 5 annotation. `spec_hash` is recomputed for all five strategies (each strategy's own pre-mortem segment and the shared router pre-mortem segment both moved); `spec_locked_since` is NOT moved for any of them. Every strategy's own Entry/Exit criteria, thresholds, indicators, router activation rules and kill criteria are unchanged by every item above.

**Rev 45 changelog (2026-08-18) — STRATEGY E GAINS A MANDATORY SHORT-LEG STOP WHOSE DISTANCE IS AI-DETERMINED PER PAIR (owner directive).** D2's 2026-08-18 session, constructing E's first pair thesis to reach the sizing step (TLN/VST, declined on E's own criteria), found that **E had no stop of any kind anywhere in its live machinery** while `Experiment_Parameters.md` §Position size, the E pre-mortem's CaR arithmetic, and rev 10's own closing disclaimer all assumed a mandatory +25% one. The only short stop the experiment had ever actually specified was **Strategy B's**, derived for B's post-event-drift horizon. E's short-leg CaR term therefore had no defensible value, and no E pair could be sized honestly. Owner directive, same day: E gets a real short-leg **exit rule**, and its distance is **not a fixed number** — *"it should be determined by the AI so it can leverage context of current market conditions to determine the best number."* Specified in full in `## Strategy E` → Exit rules. **Rails:** set at entry before sizing (an unset distance blocks entry); frozen for the life of the position, tightenable only as part of a full pair exit, never widenable; recorded with its justification in the thesis decision-log entry (`fields.short_stop_pct`, `fields.short_stop_rationale`). The stop closes the PAIR, never one leg. **Consistent with, not an exception to, Rev 43 / rev 19:** the experiment already replaced fixed sizing with AI-set, seven-factor-justified, adversarially-attacked risk budgets carrying no numeric ceiling; this extends the same posture to the one coefficient that was still expected to be a constant. **Unchanged:** every E entry criterion, every other E exit rule, E's router rule, its declared frequency, its kill criteria, and B's own 25% stop (which is B's machinery and is explicitly NOT imported into E). This is an in-life machinery edit applied by owner directive under the Rev 42 / §5.6a channel — `spec_hash` recomputed, `spec_locked_since` NOT moved.

**Rev 45 companion (2026-08-18, owner directive) — STRATEGY E ENTRY CRITERION 5's "ANCHOR 2" IS DROPPED.** The dual-anchor / ">= 10 qualifying reference pairs" gate existed **only** in `strategy/08_pre_mortems.md` prose. It was never in this document's Entry criteria and never in `strategy_math/strategy_e.py`, whose `financing_cost_ok()` implements only the single "<= 15% of thesis expected return" test that criterion 5 states here. It was introduced during a pre-mortem revision by the SL2 route-(B) path, which is documentary-only and explicitly forbidden from altering entry criteria — yet it was then enforced as a hard NO-GO gate, and on 2026-08-18 it was one of three independent grounds for declining TLN/VST, having been found unsatisfiable for the ENTIRE GICS 551050 group (4 names clear the $10B/ADV filter, ceiling C(4,2) = 6 pairs against a required 10 — a property of the industry group, not of any candidate). Owner directive: **drop it.** Criterion 5 reverts to exactly what this document has always specified and what the code has always implemented — removing an accretion that was never legitimately adopted, not weakening a ratified control.

**Rev 43 / Experiment Parameters rev 18 changelog (2026-07-28), amended by rev 19 (2026-08-05) — FIXED 2% POSITION SIZING AND BOTH SUCCESSOR CaR ENVELOPES RETIRED (owner directives).** The AI sets each thesis's Capital-at-Risk budget with no numeric ceiling. The surviving discipline is a recorded seven-factor justification plus the mandatory adversarial attack on size. Canonical specification: `Experiment_Parameters.md` rev 19 §Position size.

**This is a FUNDAMENTAL change** — it alters position-sizing methodology, one of the five material-structural-difference dimensions — and is applied through the **owner-directive channel, which the Immutability clause leaves unbounded**. It is expressly **NOT** an `AI_Trading_Foundation.md` §5.6a non-fundamental in-life edit and must not be cited as precedent for one.

**Why.** Two reasons, in order of force. **(1) The 2% rule had already stopped being a risk cap.** Rev 40 (2026-07-21) authorised adds as unbounded fresh 2% tranches with "no cumulative cap on the number of tranches a single name may accumulate" — from that date the rule governed *tranche granularity*, not per-name risk, and its derivation ("a 10-trade consecutive losing streak costs approximately 18%") silently assumed independent 2% bets that no longer existed. The document was asserting a discipline the mechanism no longer delivered. **(2) A blanket number is the wrong instrument when sizing IS the risk control.** This experiment uses no price-based stop-losses — positions run to thesis completion or invalidation — so the size decision is the only lever bounding the loss when a thesis is wrong. One fixed number applies identical risk to a tightly-falsifiable three-week catalyst and a diffuse three-year structural thesis.

**What replaces it.** Per thesis: an AI-chosen **Capital at Risk** budget (long equity = full notional, since with no stop the honest worst case is total loss; Strategy C = `max_loss` = `max(base, early-assignment cascade)`, whose base component is dual-path-verified and exact while the cascade component is single-path and rests on the 2× implied-move assumption (Rev 44, 2026-08-10 scope correction — see the Strategy C eligibility rule); short = notional × that strategy's short stop distance — **Strategy B's is its fixed 25%; Strategy E's is AI-determined per pair at entry and frozen for the position's life, Rev 45, 2026-08-18** — ; Strategy E = both legs, sized to the hedge ratio and counted once). The budget must be **justified in the decision-log entry** against the seven-factor list in `Experiment_Parameters.md` (falsifiability, time to resolution, instrument loss profile, book correlation, regime fragility, liquidity, ordinal conviction) and **adversarially attacked on size as well as direction** by each strategy's existing counter-argument entry criterion. Conviction enters as an **ordinal tier only** — never a probability multiplied into a sizing formula (`AI_Trading_Foundation.md` 3a.1, 2.26).

~~**Hard envelopes (versioned policy — ruin prevention, not conviction shaping):** per-name aggregate CaR **≤ 10%** of strategy portfolio across all tranches (this is what finally bounds Rev 40's unbounded adds — the tranche *count* stays uncapped, the total *risk* does not); per-strategy deployed CaR **≤ 75%**~~ **[owner directive 2026-08-05 — BOTH envelopes RETIRED; no numeric sizing ceiling remains at any level, so Rev 40's adds are once again unbounded in aggregate. See Experiment_Parameters.md rev 19.]**; book-level circuit breaker unchanged (−15% soft / −40% hard); Strategy C's defined-risk rail unchanged.

**Retained deliberately:** Strategy B's short stop (close if the underlying rises ≥ 25% from short-entry) is **mandatory and now load-bearing** — long downside is bounded at −100% of notional so sizing alone caps it, but short downside is unbounded, so without this stop "risk is capped by sizing" would be false for a short. Also unchanged: D's 30%-of-NAV GICS-sector exposure cap, D's minimum-5 position floor, every mechanical kill trigger, and the 30-trade gate.

**Consequences recorded.** Per-trade metrics normalise **per unit of capital at risk** (EV per trade especially); count-based metrics (hit rate, convergence rate) and deployed TWR are unaffected. `analytics.strategy_nav.sizing_base_2pct` becomes a legacy reference figure rather than "the" size budget. **All five pre-mortems cite the 2% cap as magnitude-only mitigation in their confluence lists; those paragraphs are now stale and the pre-mortems are re-opened** under §5.2 step 3 rather than left asserting a mitigation that no longer exists in that form — A3 enqueues them. `strategy_math/common.py:position_size_dollars` and `c_options_math.py:size_position` now **require** an explicit budget (the 0.02 default was removed so a forgetful caller fails loudly instead of silently reinstating 2%) and ~~both reject a budget above the 10% envelope~~ **[owner directive 2026-08-05 — NO LONGER TRUE: both functions' upper bound was widened to the domain check `(0, 1]`, i.e. no envelope ceiling at all. The `require an explicit budget` half above IS still true and unchanged.]**. **Calibration upside:** under the flat rule, overconfidence (2.13/2.26) was unmitigated *and* unmeasurable in the sizing dimension; with conviction-scaled sizing, size-vs-outcome becomes a directly measurable calibration series.

**Rev 42 changelog (2026-07-28) — NON-FUNDAMENTAL IN-LIFE STRATEGY EDIT PATH (owner directive).** Per explicit owner instruction (2026-07-28), a foundation-driven constraint edit that changes **none** of the five material-structural-difference dimensions ({strategy approach, instrument scope, position-sizing methodology, regime-router structure, kill-criteria structure}) does not alter what a strategy fundamentally is and **may be applied in life without terminating the strategy** — see the amended Immutability clause below and `AI_Trading_Foundation.md` §5.6a for the full specification. **Rationale:** immutability is a *means* to a clean, uncontaminated statistical read, and that premise is already spent by a factor the owner controls and exercises — the model of record is changed mid-strategy regardless of whether the strategy has reached an adequate trade count for analysis (`AI_Trading_Foundation.md` 2.9: calibration "does not transfer cleanly to its successor"; Part 4 step 4 flips Tier 2 magnitudes to version-pending on every model change). A series already discontinuous at owner-driven model boundaries is not protected by refusing a bounded, exogenously-triggered edit; the right control is to bound which edits are permitted and to mark the seam. **Origin:** the A2 2026 Per-Strategy Constraint Audit (`Annual_Constraint_Audit.md` finding F-1) found that a purely numerical foundation-driven relaxation was forbidden in place by the rev-37 immutability clause *and* rejected as a restart by the restart constraints for differing "in more than just threshold numbers" — a closed loop with no legal exit. **Rails (all six required, default-REJECT on ambiguity):** exogenous trigger only, with an explicit parameter-fishing prohibition (**any justification referencing the strategy's own realized P&L, hit rate or drawdown is forbidden**); loosen-only, not beyond the §5.6 computed value; per-position sizing and kill-trigger structure excluded; a dated epoch stamp marking the measurement seam (`spec_locked_since` is NOT moved); one edit per strategy per annual cycle; CI-enforced `spec_hash` + `revision_history` provenance per `scripts/check_roster_consistency.py` R-F. **Unchanged:** a change touching ≥1 of the five dimensions remains terminate-and-restart; the parameter-fishing prohibition; the globally-immutable set (~~2%-of-sub-portfolio sizing~~ **[SUPERSEDED by Rev 43 and amended by rev 19 (2026-08-05): fixed sizing and both successor CaR envelopes are retired; the live discipline is the recorded seven-factor justification plus the mandatory adversarial attack on size, with no numeric ceiling]**, the mechanical kill-trigger structure, the measurement-and-selection machinery); and the **owner-directive channel, which remains unbounded** — §5.6a governs only what an autonomous routine may do without a directive, exactly as Rev 35/36/38/39/40/41 were applied by directive. No existing strategy's thresholds, indicators or templates change under this revision.

**Rev 37 changelog (2026-07-10) — STRATEGY ARSENAL AUTONOMY CONVERSION (owner directive; SISA).** Per explicit owner instruction (2026-07-10, logged in `Decision_Log.md`, "Strategy addition/deletion made fully autonomous"), strategy-roster membership ceases to be frozen by blanket immutability and becomes **versioned policy** governed by the fully autonomous Self-Improving Strategy Arsenal (SISA) lifecycle (routines SL1–SL5 per `Claude_Task_Plan.md`; single source of truth `strategy/roster.yaml`, mirrored to `events.strategy_lifecycle` → `state.strategy_roster` → `state.active_strategy_codes`). This directive mirrors the 2026-06 capital-allocation pivot (`Experiment_Parameters.md`) that already carved allocation out of blanket immutability into versioned policy, and follows the D2a autonomous-cutover discipline (objective BigQuery readiness view + durable idempotency marker + self-executing repo edit/commit/push + info-severity audit alert, never a chat question — `ops/RUNBOOK.md` §36). **Two-tier immutability (see the Immutability clause below):** each strategy's OWN machinery stays immutable for that strategy's entire life, frozen at SHADOW entry (`spec_locked_since`), with its official edge-measurement clock (`immutable_since`) starting at its first PROBE trade; roster MEMBERSHIP and N are versioned policy. **Adding a new strategy is additive** — a new `## Strategy <code>` section, one new Boolean router activation line, and optionally one new single-strategy mechanical measurement (precedent: D's Sustained-Inversion Flag) — and is explicitly **NOT** a "revision" of any existing strategy; it no longer ends the experiment, and touches no existing strategy's thresholds. **The owner-directive override channel is generalized:** roster changes now flow through the routine-executable SISA authorization (`ops/autonomy_levels.yaml` `strategy_arsenal` = `active_auto`; compensating controls = adversarial pre-mortem + SHADOW/PAPER forward-test + concurrent-incubation cap + adoption rate limit + N≥2 floor / N≤8 ceiling + default-no/default-KEEP gates + `ops.arsenal_control` kill switch, in lieu of human PR review), and explicit owner directives remain a valid but no-longer-exclusive channel, reserved in practice for changes to the measurement-and-selection MACHINERY itself. **Unchanged:** every existing strategy's thresholds/indicators/templates; the shared regime-vocabulary thresholds; 2%-of-sub-portfolio position sizing *(rev 2026-07-28 — **SUPERSEDED by Rev 43**: the fixed 2% rule is retired in favour of thesis-scaled risk budgeting with hard envelopes, owner directive. This historical Rev 37 record is otherwise unchanged.)*; every mechanical kill trigger (drawdown / 30-trade / m2m); and the $2,000 probe-stake floor. Autonomous deletion adds only a REMOVE-ONLY, default-KEEP discretionary-retirement path (SL4) layered on top of the unchanged mechanical kills — it can never spare or continue a strategy a mechanical trigger flagged. See `Experiment_Parameters.md`, `Operating_Protocols.md`, and `ops/RUNBOOK.md` for the full lifecycle, rails, and audit surface.

**Rev 35 changelog (2026-05-30) — HOLDINGS-COUNT CAPS REMOVED (owner directive).** Per explicit owner instruction (2026-05-30), all caps on the *number* of holdings/positions a strategy may carry are removed across Strategies A, B, C, and D.
