Rev 3 spot-edit fixes:

- (T2.A) **Anchor 2 deterministic reference-set rule.** Constraint 4 / entry criterion 5 Anchor 2 reformulated: reference set is all pairs (X, Y) in the same 6-digit GICS industry group as the candidate L/S, where (a) trailing-252-day correlation between X and Y ≥ 0.5, (b) both X and Y meet the strategy's market-cap and ADV minimums, (c) the pair has at least 5 years of joint trading history. The reference set is computed by classical-method delegation; *no Claude pair-shortlisting step.* Reference set size must be ≥ 10 qualifying pairs for Anchor 2 to apply; if fewer pairs qualify (e.g., narrow industry groups with limited eligible names), Anchor 2 is unavailable for this thesis and the gate cannot clear via Anchor 2 — the pair defers entirely. Anchor 2 thus becomes statistically independent of Anchor 1 (Claude's expected return) at the input-selection level, restoring the dual-anchor architecture's bounding property.

- (T2.B) **Quantitative-divergence-anchor threshold tightened to 95th percentile (z ≈ 2σ).** Entry criterion 3 reformulated: pair's current spread must be at minimum the 95th percentile of its trailing-252-day distribution. Threshold derived from pair-trading literature standard (Gatev/Goetzmann/Rouwenhorst 2006 distance method; Avellaneda/Lee 2010 statistical arbitrage) — z ≈ 2σ is the standard divergence detection threshold for pair-trading entry, anchoring the threshold in published methodology rather than asserted choice. **Spread definition mechanism-enforced (rev 3 added):** classical-method delegation computes the choice — price-ratio for pairs where max(L price, S price) / min(L price, S price) ≤ 3 (similar share-price scales); log-spread otherwise (materially different scales). Choice rule-bound at the share-price-ratio test, not Claude-judgment-based. Reference window remains 252 trading days, explicitly stated.

- (T2.C) **Constraint 3 dual-component flowing limitation.** Entry criterion 1 tightened: "specific public events that would cause reconvergence" must be reduced to *quantitative thresholds* (e.g., "Q3 earnings revenue beat ≥ 5%" not "Q3 earnings beat"; "monthly active users grow ≥ 10% YoY for 2 consecutive quarters" not "user growth"; "FDA approval for indication X by 2027-Q3" not "FDA approval"). The threshold must be specified at thesis-formation and immutable through life. *Constraint 3 flowing limitation expanded to dual-component:* (a) entry criterion 3 quantitative-divergence-anchor at divergence-existence layer; (b) entry criterion 1 quantitative-reconvergence-threshold requirement at narrative-validity layer. Component (b) reduces the narrative's predictive content to a falsifiable quantitative claim that is session-stable (the threshold is a written number) and externally-verifiable post-hoc (the public event either does or does not meet the threshold). 2.24 weight-level convergence on narrative validity is constrained by requiring narrative to commit to a specific quantitative prediction, even though it doesn't eliminate weight-level bias on the prediction itself.

- (T2.D) **Logging specification added to Section 6.** Per-pair logging requirements specified inline: at thesis formation, Decision_Log.md entry must include pair_id (LtickerSticker format), spread_definition (price_ratio | log_spread), reference_window (252-day default), percentile_at_entry (computed value 0.95-1.00) **[rev 13, 2026-08-25 — historical figure; see Section 6's current field list above for the live 0.00-1.00 range, criterion 3's percentile gate having been dropped by owner directive]**, anchor_2_reference_set_size (count of qualifying pairs in Anchor 2 set), reconvergence_thresholds (the specific quantitative thresholds per entry criterion 1). At pair close, Portfolio_Ledger.md entry must include pair_id, realized_pnl ($ and %), holding_days, exit_reason (convergence | invalidation | timeline | correlation_breakdown) **[rev 12 correction, 2026-08-24 — the live enum now carries a fifth value, `short_leg_stop`, closing the Rev 45 short-leg-stop logging gap (AR_orc `premortem-E-2026-a3` cycle 11, item T1-2); see Section 6's current field list and Section 1's Exit rules above.]**. The entry-percentile-vs-realized-P&L rank correlation indicator computes Spearman rank correlation across the trailing 10 closed pairs at each monthly review; uncomputable until 10 pairs have closed.

#### 1. Strategy E thesis and mechanism

**Thesis.** Within a single GICS industry group (6-digit level), AI's cross-document synthesis (edges 1.1 narrative synthesis and 1.4 cross-report contradiction surfacing — 1.4 used as a synthesis technique within 1.1 per Strategy C cycle 4 T1.C precedent) and cross-disciplinary integration (edge 1.10) identifies two companies L (laggard) and S (leader) where the *narrative divergence* in public disclosures and analyst reaction patterns has gotten ahead of the *fundamental divergence*. Long L / short S captures mean reversion without net market exposure; the pair structure is intended to compensate AI_Trading_Foundation 2.7 (regime maladaptation) and 2.20 (textbook-rational penalty) since market direction is structurally neutralized for the pair's returns. *Compensation claims engaged in Section 5 with explicit residuals and the router-deactivation contradiction (rev 2 added per cycle 1 T1.6).*

**Instrument eligibility rule.**
- Long L + Short S, both same GICS industry group (6-digit level)
- Both legs are executable at one AI-chosen combined risk budget, sized to the hedge ratio. E's CaR is the sum of both legs' CaR: long-leg CaR is full notional; short-leg CaR is notional × the short-leg stop distance set for that pair at entry. **[rev 11 correction, owner directive 2026-08-18 — this bullet previously stated the coefficient as a "mandatory +25% stop distance." No such stop existed anywhere in E's machinery until today: `Strategy.md`'s Entry/Exit criteria never specified one, `strategy_math/strategy_e.py`'s `financing_cost_ok()` computes only the 15%-of-expected-return financing test and defines no stop distance anywhere in the module, and the only short stop the experiment ever specified was Strategy B's 25%, derived for B's own post-event-drift horizon and never extended to E. This pre-mortem asserted a bound that did not exist, unchanged from rev 1 through rev 10 (2026-04-25 through 2026-08-17); D2 found the gap on 2026-08-18 at E's first thesis to reach the sizing step (`events.decision_log` ops-note `5f65959b-d259-47e7-966e-e0b61f9ccbde`), and owner directive Rev 45 closed it the same day. The CaR identity itself — short-leg CaR = short notional × stop distance; pair budget = sum of both legs' CaR — was always correct; only its coefficient was fictional. **The real rule, now live in `Strategy.md`'s `## Strategy E` → Exit rules:** the distance is AI-determined per pair at entry from that pair's own measured conditions (S's realized/implied volatility, the pair's trailing-252-day spread distribution, the 252-day correlation and hedge ratio, borrow cost and expected holding period, and the prevailing regime) — not a fixed constant. **Three rails:** set BEFORE sizing (an unset distance blocks entry, since short-leg CaR cannot be computed without it); frozen for the life of the position (may tighten only as part of a full pair exit, never widen); recorded with its justification in the thesis's `events.decision_log` entry (`fields.short_stop_pct`, `fields.short_stop_rationale`). The stop closes the PAIR, not the short leg alone.]** Under rev 19 the combined budget has no numeric ceiling. The seven-factor justification is mandatory; a market-neutral pair is not risk-neutral in the CaR sense. **[rev 8 correction, cycle 8 W3, per AR_orc cycle-7 finding]** The prior text here also named an "entry-criterion-2 adversarial size attack" as a mandatory compensating control; struck, since entry criterion 2 (Section 1, criterion 2) is a narrative adversarial-counter-argument step whose full definition never mentions size, CaR, or sizing, and no distinct size-specific adversarial mechanism is defined anywhere in this section — see Known Limitation 18, which registers the gap and its machinery-level escalation. **[rev 8 addition, cycle 7 AR_orc W5]** The seven factors, reproduced from `Experiment_Parameters.md` rev 18 §Position size for self-containment: (1) falsifiability of the invalidation criteria — tighter, more observable, nearer-dated invalidation supports a larger budget; (2) time to resolution — longer horizons accumulate unknown-unknowns and regime risk, supporting a smaller budget; (3) instrument loss profile — defined-risk exposure supports a larger budget than open-ended equity downside at equal conviction; (4) correlation with the existing book — a thesis duplicating already-held exposure supports a smaller budget; (5) regime fragility — smaller sizing where the router's activation is marginal or the thesis depends on a regime the foundation flags as adverse; (6) liquidity and exit-ability at the intended size; (7) conviction, ordinal only — never an explicit probability multiplied into a sizing formula, per 2.13/2.26. The justification is recorded in the thesis's `events.decision_log` entry; a size with no recorded justification is not a valid order. *Historical rev43/rev7 finding:* the then-live 10% per-name / 75% deployed envelopes made a pair's two-name exposure materially larger than a single-name template; rev 7 corrected that envelope-era arithmetic. Those envelopes are retired, not current controls.
- Each leg counts as 1 position toward the 30-trade gate; a pair equals 2 trades
- ETF-pair substitution permitted when individual-stock shorting infeasible at current portfolio size (S's share price × minimum-share-count × ~~2% cap~~ **the pair's AI-chosen risk budget (Rev 43, owner directive, 2026-07-28)** interact unfavorably). ETF-pair execution acknowledged as diluting idiosyncratic thesis; documented accepted cost (KL #9). Substitution becomes unnecessary as portfolio grows past threshold.

**Deployment posture.** E has no numeric deployed-CaR or per-name ceiling under rev 19. Actual deployment follows the open pairs' independently justified budgets; any remainder is parked. Deployed TWR is measured on deployed capital only.

**Entry criteria (all must be met):**

1. **Narrative divergence thesis identified** with specific public-information basis for why L is under-narrated relative to S, with explicit predictions about what public events would cause reconvergence (specific reconvergence indicators must be named at entry, immutable through life). **Quantitative-reconvergence-threshold requirement (rev 3 added per cycle 2 T2.C):** the "specific public events that would cause reconvergence" must be reduced to quantitative thresholds — e.g., "Q3 earnings revenue beat ≥ 5%" rather than "Q3 earnings beat"; "monthly active users grow ≥ 10% YoY for 2 consecutive quarters" rather than "user growth strengthens"; "FDA approval for indication X by 2027-Q3" rather than "FDA approval." The threshold must be specified at thesis-formation and immutable through life. This reduces the narrative's predictive content to a falsifiable quantitative claim that is session-stable (the threshold is a written number, not a subjective judgment) and externally-verifiable post-hoc (the public event either does or does not meet the threshold). Component of Constraint 3 dual-component flowing limitation (narrative-validity layer); see Constraint 3.

2. **Adversarial counter-argument** doesn't identify a decisive flaw — the same single Claude session producing the thesis runs the counter-argument before commitment. Acknowledged as graded discriminator (~30% empirical reduction per 2.4) **[rev 14, 2026-08-25 — this ~30% is UNSOURCED; see the rev-12 provenance flag at Section 5's 2.4 entry for the evidence, which is not repeated here. AR_orc `premortem-E-2026-a3` cycle 11 T1-1 sustained the figure as unsourced across "at least four" recurrences in this section, and rev 12 annotated only ONE of them — the Section 5 2.4 locus — leaving this one, Constraint 2's and Known Limitation 1's standing as though established. EP states its own consequences in terms this criterion inherits: *"A size that survives no adversarial attack is not sized, it is asserted,"* and *"a size with no recorded justification is not a valid order."* **(b) The consequence of a negative verdict, now stated.** A size attack concluding that the loss is **not acceptable** is a decisive flaw *within this criterion's own words*: the criterion requires that no decisive flaw be identified, the size of the pair is part of what is being committed, and a loss the attack itself judges unacceptable is not a lesser finding than a directional one. Therefore **criterion 2 is NOT satisfied at that size, and the entry is not admissible at that size.** The AI may re-derive a smaller risk budget and re-run the attack against the new figure — any re-sized budget carries its own seven-factor justification recorded in the thesis's `events.decision_log` entry — but committing at a size the attack has judged unacceptable is barred. There is no advisory reading under which the pair is entered unchanged. **(c) What this does NOT do.** It adds no gate, threshold, computation or rule, and bounds nothing about how large a budget the attack may clear — post-rev-19 there is no numeric ceiling, and the attack remains a same-model check on a same-model decision, graded rather than eliminative (the ~30% figure above, and Constraint 2 / 2.4, are unchanged). It is not code-enforced: nothing reads a size-attack verdict, so this binds the authoring session's discipline and is auditable after the fact from `events.decision_log` — which is exactly what the Known Limitation 18 review trigger, itemized in Section 6 by this revision, checks. E's own criterion-2 **text** in `Strategy.md`'s live Entry criteria still does not restate the extension; amending it there is machinery and **stays escalated (KL18's transmission residual).]**

3. **Classical-method delegation produces:**

4. **Expected holding period** to thesis resolution is 1-6 months (matches the time-based exit window at criterion 5).

5. **Short-financing cost gate.** Computed by code (`strategy_math/strategy_e.py: financing_cost_ok()`): borrow rate × position size × expected holding period, and is ≤ 15% of Claude's articulated thesis expected return — otherwise financing eats the alpha. This is the entire gate; it is the single test `Strategy.md`'s Entry criteria have always specified, and the only test `financing_cost_ok()` has ever implemented. **[rev 11 correction, owner directive 2026-08-18 — this criterion previously stated a "dual-anchor" gate: the test above relabeled "Anchor 1," plus a second, deterministic "Anchor 2" requiring financing cost to also clear 15% of a trailing-252-day historical-median pair-mean-reversion magnitude computed across a reference set of all pairs (X, Y) in the candidate's own 6-digit GICS industry group meeting a ≥0.5 trailing-252-day correlation, the strategy's market-cap (≥$10B) and ADV (≥$20M) minimums, and ≥5 years of joint trading history, with a ≥10-qualifying-pair minimum for Anchor 2 to apply — failing that minimum deferred the thesis entirely, with no override. Anchor 2 existed only in this pre-mortem's prose: it was introduced at rev 3 (2026-04-25, cycle 2 T2.A) via SL2's route (B), which is documentary-only and explicitly forbidden from altering entry criteria; it was never in `Strategy.md`'s Entry criteria, and `financing_cost_ok()` has never implemented it. Despite that, it was enforced as a hard NO-GO gate from rev 3 forward, and on 2026-08-18 it was one of three independent grounds for declining TLN/VST after being found unsatisfiable for the ENTIRE GICS 551050 group (9 names; 4 clear the $10B-market-cap/$20M-ADV filter; pairing ceiling C(4,2) = 6 against a required 10 — a property of the industry group's membership, not of the candidate's price action; the arithmetic finding itself remains true and is kept as a recorded observation at KL #15 even though the gate that made it binding is gone). Owner directive, same day: drop it — an accretion that was never legitimately adopted into frozen machinery, not a ratified control being weakened. Criterion 5 above reverts to exactly what `Strategy.md` and the code have always specified. See Constraint 4 for the residual this leaves on the expected-return input.]**

6. **Public-information compliance check.** During thesis construction, if reasoning requires any phrase resembling "per industry contacts," "sources familiar with," "per channel checks," "management has indicated privately," or equivalent — thesis is not tradeable. Session must abandon thesis. (Constraint 1 flowing limitation; sell-side handling clause per Strategy C rev 19 precedent — see Constraint 1 for full text.)

**Pair-prioritization rule (rev 4 added per cycle 3 T1.A).** ~~When multiple candidate pairs simultaneously meet entry criterion 3's quantitative-divergence-anchor threshold (≥ 95th percentile of trailing-252-day distribution), Claude evaluates them for thesis construction in deterministic rank order: descending by spread-percentile-magnitude (highest percentile evaluated first; ties broken by candidate's earlier first-trigger-date if available, else alphabetical by pair_id).~~ **[rev 13, owner directive 2026-08-25 — restated: entry criterion 3's ≥95th-percentile threshold is DROPPED as a gate (see entry criterion 3), so it can no longer be this rule's trigger. The rule's original intent was always ordering, not admission, and that intent is unchanged: among the candidate pairs that meet entry criterion 3 as it now reads (L-S correlation over trailing 252 trading days ≥ 0.5; beta-adjusted leg sizing), Claude evaluates them for thesis construction in deterministic rank order, prioritized by measured spread percentile — descending by spread-percentile-magnitude, highest percentile evaluated first; ties broken by candidate's earlier first-trigger-date if available, else alphabetical by `pair_id`. The percentile used for ranking is the same measured, recorded `percentile_at_entry` value described at entry criterion 3 — computed and logged for every candidate regardless of admission — so this restatement requires no new machinery, only a restated trigger.]** Ranking computed by classical-method delegation across the full candidate pool at the start of each thesis-construction session. *No skip-and-prioritize step is permitted* — Claude must evaluate the top-ranked candidate before moving to the next; if it fails any criterion 1-6 gate, Claude proceeds to the next-ranked candidate. The rule eliminates Claude-judgment pair-prioritization, which would otherwise be a 2.4 (narrative-attractiveness selection) and 2.24 (session-variable but weight-bias-correlated prioritization) exposed selection step upstream of all downstream gates. Cycle 3 T1.A surfaced that rev 3's tightened 95th-percentile gate created a higher-stakes admissibility test without addressing the prioritization layer above it; rev 4 closes the gap by rule-binding the prioritization decision. *Note for Section 1 declared universe (rev 4 cross-reference per cycle 3 T2.J):* the operating universe is further restricted to pairs that pass Constraint 3 component (b) — theses without quantitative reconvergence thresholds (genuinely qualitative reconvergence mechanisms like "cultural momentum cycles") are excluded.

**Exit rules.**
- Convergence target reached (specific public reconvergence indicators per criterion 1 met).
- Thesis invalidation: new public information changes fundamental situation on either leg (management change, material operational event, M&A announcement, regulatory action).
- **Time-based exit at 6 months:** thesis stale; short-financing costs accumulating; exit regardless of P&L. *Rev 2 acknowledgment per cycle 1 T1.13:* this rule operates 2.18 instruction-adherence-over-capital-preservation in a structurally-bounded form — capital is occasionally sacrificed to rule preservation when a thesis is mid-resolution at month 7. Trade-off accepted: stale-thesis defense (preventing indefinite hold of a no-longer-converging pair with accumulating financing drag) outweighs the single-thesis cost of premature exit. Documented as KL #5.
- **Correlation breakdown:** rolling 60-day correlation between L and S falls below 0.3 (pair relationship broken).
- **Short-leg stop — MANDATORY, distance AI-determined per pair at entry (Rev 45, owner directive, 2026-08-18; mirrored here by rev 12, 2026-08-24, closing the AR_orc `premortem-E-2026-a3` cycle-11 T1-2 finding and the deferral rev 11 recorded above).** Close the pair if S rises from its short-entry price by at least the stop distance fixed for this pair at entry. Three non-waivable rails: (1) set at entry, BEFORE sizing — an unset distance blocks entry; (2) frozen for the life of the position — tightenable only as part of a full pair exit, never widenable; (3) recorded with its justification in the thesis's `events.decision_log` entry (`fields.short_stop_pct`, `fields.short_stop_rationale`). The stop closes the PAIR, not the short leg alone. Full specification — including the factors the distance is derived from and why it is not a fixed constant — is `Strategy.md`'s `## Strategy E` → Exit rules, the governing text.

Not exit-triggering: adverse mark-to-market with no thesis-invalidating news; general market moves (pair approximately neutral by construction).

**Declared expected frequency.** 6-12 pair theses per year over active periods. Each pair = 2 trades. Equivalent to 12-24 trades/year toward the 30-trade gate. At declared rate, the gate is reachable in ~15-30 months of active operation. *Rev 2 reconciliation per cycle 1 T1.11 sub-item:* the prior rev 1 "20 positions = directional signal floor" reference was conflating positions and pair-closes; clarification: directional signal floor matches the experiment-level 30-trade gate, which corresponds to 15 pair-closes for E.

**Router activation rule.** Technical: (SPY Trend State ≠ DOWN) AND (VIX Regime ≠ HIGH) AND (Breadth State = HEALTHY). Rationale: in DOWN regimes and HIGH VIX regimes, within-sector correlations collapse toward 1 (everything moves together in fear) or toward 0 (everything unmoored) — either extreme breaks pair mechanics. The HEALTHY breadth requirement reflects E's need for cross-sectional dispersion within each sector. *Cycle 1 T1.6 contradiction acknowledged in Section 5 / Constraint compensation engagement:* the router-deactivation regimes are the regimes where 2.7 (regime maladaptation) would actually bite, so the strategy mechanism's market-neutrality compensation claim coexists with a router rule that disallows the strategy precisely when 2.7 binds. The compensation claim is operative within the activated-regime band only.

#### 2. Systematic money-loss scenarios

1. **Within-sector correlation collapse during stress.** SPY DOWN or VIX HIGH causes sector correlations to diverge to extremes (toward 1 or toward 0); pair mechanics fail. *Rev 2 expansion:* the router excludes these regimes for new entries, but existing positions held when regime shifts mid-trade are exposed. The 60-day rolling correlation < 0.3 exit triggers but only after 60 days of breakdown.
2. **Structural disruption on one leg mid-trade.** Management turnover, M&A announcement, major product failure, or regulatory action at L or S changes the pair relationship. Thesis was about narrative divergence; company-level structural change isn't covered.
3. **Pseudo-narratives (per 2.4).** AI finds compelling "L is the unloved laggard, S is over-narrated" stories that don't map to actual return relationships. Narrative coherence without predictive power is exactly 2.4's territory.
4. **Short-financing cost erosion.** Borrow rates spike on S during holding period; short leg accumulates costs that consume the pair's alpha. Rate changes post-entry are unconstrained by the entry-time financing gate.
5. **Pair turns unprofitable on financing alone with no thesis movement (rev 2 added per cycle 1 T1.10).** At entry the pair clears the 15% financing gate; over the holding period, accumulated financing drag exceeds any narrative-driven convergence so the pair closes at negative P&L despite thesis playing out.
6. **Small-cap or illiquid execution forces ETF substitution.** When portfolio size below threshold, ETF substitution required; ETF pairs dilute idiosyncratic thesis (ETFs carry many constituents); E's edge is in the narrative divergence on specific names, not on sector-rotation pairs. Documented residual.
7. **Expected-return overestimation distorts the 15% financing gate (rev 2 added per cycle 1 T1.5 / 2.13/2.26).** Claude's expected-return forecast for the pair is persistently overestimated per 2.13/2.26's hard-wired bias. A miscalibrated numerator inflates the gate's permissiveness; pairs that should be rejected on financing economics pass because the expected return is overstated. **[rev 11 correction, owner directive 2026-08-18 — "mitigated by rev 2 dual-anchor gate" struck: that second anchor never existed outside this pre-mortem's prose and is removed by owner directive, see entry criterion 5.]** Unmitigated; residual structural overconfidence at the financing gate.
8. **Look-ahead contamination on pair-historical analysis (rev 2 added per cycle 1 T1.5 / 2.19).** Pair-trading is textbook-extensively-documented; specific pair episodes' convergence outcomes likely encoded in training data. The 252-day correlation, 60-day rolling correlation, and beta-adjustment computations are mechanism-enforced (classical-method delegation), but pair-selection itself draws on Claude's reasoning about which pairs have historically mean-reverted — directly in 2.19's hot zone.
9. **6-month forced exit on mid-resolution thesis (rev 2 acknowledged per cycle 1 T1.13).** Time-based exit at 6 months fires regardless of whether thesis is approaching convergence. Per 2.18 territory.
10. **2.7/2.20 compensation claim failure during stress (rev 2 acknowledged per cycle 1 T1.6).** Market-neutrality at thesis formation does not guarantee market-neutrality during the hold — beta drifts, factor exposure leaks. Both names can price at fundamentals tightly under 2.20 with the *spread* itself being textbook-rational-undervalued in bubble regimes (the divergence is what 2.20 underprices). Compensation is contingent within router-activated regimes only.

#### 3. SGOV-underperformance scenarios

1. **Thesis works but slowly.** Short-financing cost accumulates faster than convergence generates profit; pair closes at small profit < SGOV yield over the same period.
2. **Pair trades at low capital scale.** Small absolute position sizes mean disproportionate commission/spread cost.
3. **Persistent ETF-substitution regime.** When portfolio size doesn't support individual-stock shorting, every pair is ETF-vs-ETF; idiosyncratic thesis dilutes; sector-rotation-pair returns historically thinner than SGOV in the regimes E operates within.
4. **STCG drag (rev 2 added per cycle 1 T2.1 / 2.23).** All E theses are 1-6 month holds, fully STCG-rate. Pair structure means every thesis is two STCG-taxed legs (L's gain and S's loss both at ordinary income rates). Tax drag is structural to E's holding period; nominal returns must materially exceed SGOV plus tax wedge to deliver positive excess real return.

#### 4. Edge-decay indicators

- **Pair convergence rate < 50% over rolling 10 closes (rev 2 derivation added per cycle 1 T1.11).** Hit defined as: pair closed at convergence target with positive net P&L. Threshold derived from pairs-trading literature reporting 50-70% convergence rate for in-sector pair strategies in normal regimes; 50% is the lower bound of normal performance baseline. **[rev 15 provenance flag, 2026-08-30, per AR_orc `premortem-E-2026-a3` cycle 12 Tier 3 — the 50–70% convergence-rate band above is UNSOURCED. "Pairs-trading literature" names no paper, no sample, no window and no method; the figure has been carried in the present tense since rev 2 (2026-04-25) with no citation ever attached, and nothing in this section or in `strategy/07_strategy_e.md` supplies one. The review offered "name the paper(s) or flag it unsourced in the manner the ~30% figure now carries." **The flag is taken and no citation is supplied**, deliberately: originating a source this section cannot verify from its own text would repeat exactly the failure the ~30% history recorded in this document was written to expose — a number that reads as established because it has been restated often enough. Read 50–70% as an unsourced working assumption, not a measurement. **The indicator and its threshold are UNAFFECTED:** the `< 50%` convergence-rate flag over rolling 10 closes stands, and the 15-pair-close calibration gate below is the mechanism that replaces the assumed baseline with E's own realized distribution — which is the honest remedy available inside this route, and the reason the figure is flagged rather than removed.]** Below 50% convergence over 10 closes = below-baseline signal. Calibration-refined at 15-pair-close gate based on realized distribution per Strategy B/C precedent.

- **Cumulative excess real return vs SGOV negative at 10 pair closes.** Standard experiment-level edge-decay metric (consistent with A/B/C/D).

- **Convergence-vs-timeline-expiry ratio < 1:1 over rolling 10 closes (rev 2 derivation added per cycle 1 T1.11).** # closed-on-convergence / # closed-on-6-month-timeline-exit. Threshold derived from same pairs-trading literature: convergence rate at 50% baseline = ratio 1:1 (1 convergence : 1 timeline expiry). Ratio below 1:1 indicates most trades aging out rather than converging — strategy not generating its thesis-resolution mechanism.

- **Portfolio-level financing drag ratio > 25% (rev 2 added per cycle 1 T1.10).** Trailing-12-month sum of borrow costs / trailing-12-month gross pair P&L (across closed positions). Threshold: > 25% → flag (financing eating ≥ 1/4 of strategy alpha). Rationale: at 25% drag, even successful pair theses have 75% of their gross alpha after-financing; at 50%+, the strategy structurally can't beat SGOV on excess basis.

- **Mean entry-time borrow cost trend rising > 50% in 12 months (rev 2 added per cycle 1 T1.10).** 6-month moving average of entry-time borrow costs across the strategy book. Trend rising > 50% in 12 months → flag. Rationale: portfolio-wide rising entry borrow rates indicates the strategy's economics worsening structurally, distinct from individual-name borrow spikes (operational risk handled at Section 6 monthly indicator).

- **Thesis-quality-vs-realized-P&L correlation discriminating signal (rev 2 reframed per cycle 1 T1.12).** Replaces rev 1's "near zero correlation between adversarial-review outcome quality and realized P&L" which was operationally vacant. Reframed as: at-entry quantitative-divergence-anchor magnitude (criterion 3 percentile rank of pair spread vs trailing 252 days) correlated with realized P&L on closed positions. If pairs entered at higher percentile ranks (closer to historical extremes) don't outperform pairs entered at lower percentile ranks, ~~the entry filter~~ **the recorded entry percentile** is not discriminating. Threshold: rank-correlation between entry-percentile and realized-P&L < 0 over rolling 10 closes → flag (~~entry filter~~ **the recorded entry percentile** is anti-discriminating). **[rev 15 label correction, 2026-08-30, per AR_orc `premortem-E-2026-a3` cycle 12 Tier 2 — both instances of "the entry filter" named the percentile as something that filters. It does not, and has not since rev 13 (owner directive) dropped criterion 3's percentile requirement as an entry condition: the value is measured and recorded at entry (`percentile_at_entry`, Section 6) and used to rank candidate pairs, but it admits and excludes nothing. **The INDICATOR ITSELF IS UNALTERED** — same Spearman rank correlation, same `percentile_at_entry` vs `realized_pnl_percent` inputs, same rolling-10-closes window, same `< 0` flag threshold, same classical-method delegation. Only the label moves, from a gate to the observation it actually is. Rev 13 recorded that this indicator SURVIVES the anchor's removal and in fact improves, because `percentile_at_entry` can now vary across its full 0.00–1.00 range instead of being clamped to 0.95–1.00; relabelling it here is that same correction reaching Section 4's prose.]** Operationally measurable: each pair's at-entry percentile is logged at thesis formation; realized P&L tracked at close; rank correlation computed by classical-method delegation.

#### 5. Specific AI_Trading_Foundation disadvantages posing greatest risk

- **2.4 (narrative over-fit on pair narratives, rev 2 expanded per cycle 1 T1.4):** *central risk for E.* Pair narratives are seductive — there's always a "better" and "worse" company in any industry group. AI's mechanism is literally narrative synthesis on which company is under-narrated; 2.4 is the on-point failure mode. Self-reference closure: the only at-entry discriminator on whether the pair narrative is over-fit is Claude's own assessment, which is biased by 2.4. *Mitigation:* entry criterion 2 (adversarial counter-argument) as graded discriminator (~30% empirical reduction per 2.4); ~~rev 2 entry criterion 3 quantitative-divergence-anchor (95th-percentile pair-spread test, rev 3 tightened from 80th) as external-to-reasoning verification.~~ **[rev 13, owner directive 2026-08-25 — DROPPED as a gate (see entry criterion 3); the spread percentile continues to be COMPUTED and RECORDED at entry as an observation (Section 6), but no longer excludes any candidate. Mitigation accordingly weakens from a filter-plus-verification to a recorded-observation only.]** ~~*Residual:* ~70% structural after counter-argument; the quantitative anchor verifies divergence magnitude exists but doesn't verify the narrative's predictive validity.~~ **[rev 13] *Residual, risen:* with the anchor no longer filtering candidates, entry criterion 2's graded adversarial counter-argument (~30% empirical reduction, itself unsourced — see the rev-12 provenance flag immediately below) is now the ONLY at-entry discriminator on whether a pair narrative is over-fit; the spread-percentile observation records divergence magnitude after the fact rather than verifying it before entry. **E's residual on 2.4 rises accordingly — a real increase in accepted risk, stated plainly, not a wording change.** **[rev 12 provenance flag, 2026-08-24, AR_orc `premortem-E-2026-a3` cycle 11 T1-1 — the ~30%/~70% split above is UNSOURCED. It originates in `AI_Trading_Foundation.md` §1.3's unattributed sentence ("the bias is only reduced by about 30% — it is hard-wired in the model's weights"), uncited since the repository's first commit (`e7be4c0`, 2026-05-08); A1's 2026-07-28 annual re-derivation searched for a source at all four evidence levels with two independent agents and recorded it ABSENT (`Annual_AI_Foundation_Sweep.md` §C.3), remedy NEW RESEARCH — not a replication on the current model line — and `AI_Trading_Foundation.md` §§1.3 and 2.4 both carry that flag verbatim. 2.4's EXISTENCE claim and the narrative-over-fit MECHANISM are unaffected; only the MAGNITUDE is unsupported. Read ~30%/~70% here as an unsourced working assumption, not a measurement. Swept identically across all five strategy pre-mortems' primary 2.4 loci in one pass rather than only this locus and Strategy C's (`premortem-C-2026-a3` cycle 15 T1-4).]** See Constraint 2.

- **2.6 (no access to private information, rev 2 expanded per cycle 1 T1.4):** *load-bearing constraint for E.* Pair theses about why one company is under-narrated relative to another are exactly where channel checks, expert calls, and management access provide professional-investor edge. *Mitigation:* universe restriction per Constraint 1 (any private-information-derived rationale invalidates the thesis at construction time). Sell-side handling clause: theses depending on private-information-class signals are inadmissible even when laundered through sell-side reports (Strategy C rev 19 precedent); thesis must be reconstructible from primary public sources without sell-side's private-information-derived conclusions as load-bearing rationale. *Residual:* (i) sell-side may inadvertently launder private-information-derived conclusions in ways Claude cannot fully audit; (ii) public-only thesis space is structurally narrower than the full edge-domain. See Constraint 1.

- **2.7 (regime maladaptation, rev 2 added per cycle 1 T1.6):** strategy thesis explicitly *claims compensation* via market-neutrality. *Mitigation:* market-neutrality of the L/S pair structurally removes net market exposure, so thesis returns don't depend on market direction. *Residual structure (rev 2 engagement of compensation claim):* (a) market-neutrality at thesis formation does not guarantee market-neutrality during the hold — beta drifts, factor exposure leaks into the pair; (b) the router rule excludes E during DOWN/HIGH-VIX/non-HEALTHY-breadth regimes, which are the regimes where 2.7 would actually bite. Compensation claim is operative within router-activated regimes only — outside them, the strategy is deactivated rather than running with compensation. The compensation claim should not be read as "2.7 fully neutralized" but as "router-activated band combined with structural neutralization addresses 2.7 within scope." *Residual:* mid-trade beta drift + within-pair factor leakage; addressed at Section 6 by 60-day-correlation-breakdown exit but not directly by a beta-drift indicator.

- **2.8 (homogenization on commoditized AI strategy):** AI-driven equity selection homogenizes; pair-trading is textbook strategy with extensively documented patterns; AI sessions converge on similar pair narratives. If Claude's narrative is consensus AI narrative, the pair is crowded. *Mitigation:* GICS-industry-group level pair construction (6-digit, narrower than sector) reduces overlap with the most-crowded sector-level pair trades; the within-industry-group focus puts E in the narrower-name space where AI consensus is less concentrated. *Residual:* cross-AI-operator crowding on individual names is unaddressed at strategy level; observable post-hoc via SGOV underperformance and edge-decay indicators.

- **2.13 (miscalibration on directional magnitudes, rev 2 added per cycle 1 T1.5):** the entry gate at "short financing cost ≤ 15% of thesis expected return" load-bears on a *directional magnitude estimate* (expected return) produced by an AI known to persistently overestimate returns. A miscalibrated numerator inflates the gate's permissiveness. *Mitigation:* **[rev 11 correction, owner directive 2026-08-18 — the "rev 2 dual-anchor gate" this bullet cited as mitigation never existed outside this pre-mortem's prose (see entry criterion 5, Constraint 4) and is removed.]** none at the financing gate itself; the graded adversarial counter-argument (entry criterion 2) and the mandatory adversarial attack on size are the surviving disciplines, and neither specifically checks the expected-return magnitude this gate divides by. *Residual:* structural and unmitigated at the financing gate — a miscalibrated numerator inflates gate permissiveness with no independent check. See Constraint 4.

- **2.15 (base-rate neglect):** within-industry-group mean reversion is not universal; AI may over-weight narrative congruence ("the L/S pair has clear divergence") and under-weight base rate that specific "better vs worse" theses don't converge in any given holding window. The pairs-trading literature reports 50-70% convergence rate; AI's narrative-driven pair selection is exposed to selecting cases where convergence rate is below baseline. *Mitigation:* none implemented at strategy level. Section 4 indicators (convergence rate < 50% threshold, convergence-vs-timeline ratio) provide post-hoc detection. *Residual:* structural; no entry-time base-rate gate.

- **2.17 (algorithm appreciation bias on classical-method outputs, rev 2 added per cycle 1 T2.2):** correlation, beta, and financing computations come back as numbers from code; 2.17 says AI overweights algorithmic authority. A pair scoring 0.51 correlation passes the entry gate; one at 0.49 fails. The discontinuity at 0.50 will be deferred-to with high confidence even when underlying estimate has wide CI. *Mitigation:* none directly implemented; ~~partially addressed by rev 2 quantitative-divergence-anchor (criterion 3, 95th percentile — rev 3 tightened from 80th) which adds a second mechanism-enforced threshold.~~ **[rev 13, owner directive 2026-08-25 — this partial mitigation is GONE. Entry criterion 3's percentile anchor no longer gates admission (see entry criterion 3): the spread percentile is still measured and recorded (Section 6), but a recorded observation is not a threshold, and 2.17 is specifically about deference to a threshold. Mitigation reverts to none.]** *Residual, risen (rev 13):* the 0.50 correlation discontinuity is now E's ONLY mechanism-enforced entry threshold, with no complementary second threshold to dilute bright-line deference to it; threshold-as-bright-line for correlation gate; ~~KL #11~~ **KL #12** **[rev 9 correction, cycle-8 AR_orc Tier 3: KL #11 is pair-correlation non-stationarity in stress vs dispersion regimes — a different failure mode that happens to touch the same 0.5 band. KL #12 ("2.17 algorithm appreciation bias on 0.50 correlation discontinuity") is the matching item; its "0.51 passes; 0.49 fails" wording is a near-verbatim echo of this bullet's own.]**

- **2.18 (instruction adherence over capital preservation, rev 2 added per cycle 1 T1.13):** the 6-month time-based exit fires regardless of P&L and regardless of whether thesis is mid-resolution. By design (stale-thesis defense), but the rule's interaction with capital preservation — exiting a mid-converging thesis in month 7 — is exactly 2.18's territory. *Mitigation:* the stale-thesis-defense purpose is explicit and bounded (6-month limit prevents indefinite financing drag on aging theses). Trade-off explicitly accepted: occasional sacrifice of single-thesis capital to rule preservation > unbounded financing drag on stalled theses. *Residual:* documented as KL #5.

- **2.19 (look-ahead bias in pre-training, rev 2 added per cycle 1 T1.5):** E's mechanism explicitly draws on historical pair behavior — 252-day correlation, 60-day rolling correlation, beta adjustment from history. Pair-trading is textbook-extensively-documented in financial literature; specific pair episodes' convergence outcomes are likely encoded in training data. *Mitigation:* the quantitative computations (correlation, beta) are mechanism-enforced via classical-method delegation, so the historical math itself is not Claude-produced. However, *pair selection* — Claude's reasoning about which pairs have historically mean-reverted, which are textbook examples — is exposed to 2.19. ~~The rev 2 quantitative-divergence-anchor (criterion 3) provides forward-anchored verification but not backward-pattern-immunity.~~ **[rev 15, 2026-08-30, per AR_orc `premortem-E-2026-a3` cycle 12 Tier 1 (iii) — STRUCK. Rev 13 (owner directive) dropped criterion 3's ≥ 95th-percentile requirement as an entry condition, so the anchor provides no verification of any kind before admission: the spread percentile is still computed and recorded (`percentile_at_entry`), but no thesis is checked against it and none is excluded by it. **Corrected statement:** the percentile is a forward-dated *observation* recorded at entry, not a forward-anchored *verification* of the pair selection — and it never supplied backward-pattern-immunity in any case, which was this sentence's only accurate half. Nothing now stands between 2.19-contaminated pair selection and admission except the same Claude reasoning the disadvantage describes; the residual below is unchanged in kind and larger in reach.]** *Residual:* pair-selection contamination on textbook pair episodes; structural.

- **2.20 (textbook-rational penalty, rev 2 added per cycle 1 T1.6):** strategy thesis explicitly *claims compensation* via market-neutrality — same compensation claim as 2.7. *Engagement of compensation claim:* market-neutrality removes the strategy's exposure to bubble formation at the *index level*, but within a market-neutral pair, both names can price at fundamentals tightly under 2.20 — the *spread* itself can be textbook-rational-undervalued in bubble regimes (the divergence is what 2.20 underprices). When the market is bubbly, the L/S spread should arguably widen because bubble dynamics push leaders further from fundamentals; AI's textbook-rational pricing produces tighter spread predictions than realized. *Residual:* compensation claim addresses index-level bubble exposure but not within-pair-spread bubble dynamics; partial.

- **2.23 (tax and fee drag — STCG structural, rev 2 added per cycle 1 T2.1):** all E theses are 1-6 month holds, fully STCG-rate. Pair structure means every thesis is two STCG-taxed legs. *Mitigation:* none possible without changing the strategy's holding window (which would change its thesis structure entirely). *Residual:* structural to E's holding period; the SGOV comparison must be done in after-tax terms. Section 3 scenario 4 documents.

- **2.24 (cross-session inconsistency, rev 2 expanded per cycle 1 T1.9):** *load-bearing for E.* Adversarial review across separate sessions is the primary thesis-quality discipline at entry. If sessions converge on similar narratives (2.24's same-weight-level-bias-cuts-across-sessions property), "passes adversarial review" tells you nothing about thesis quality — the apparent agreement is weight-level convergence, not genuine independence. E's edge architecture is compromised by the same property it relies on for edge generation. ~~*Mitigation:* rev 2 entry criterion 3 quantitative-divergence-anchor provides external-to-Claude-reasoning verification of pair-spread divergence magnitude. The quantitative anchor doesn't address whether the *narrative explanation* of the divergence is valid (which is still Claude-reasoning), but it grounds the divergence's *existence* in a quantitative fact.~~ **[rev 15, 2026-08-30, per AR_orc `premortem-E-2026-a3` cycle 12 Tier 1 (ii) — demoted, in the manner rev 13 used at Constraint 3 component (a). Entry criterion 3's percentile requirement no longer gates admission (rev 13, owner directive), so it "provides verification" of nothing reachable before entry: nothing is checked against it and no candidate pair is excluded by it. **What SURVIVES, stated precisely, because rev 13 deliberately preserved it and a blanket strike here would have deleted it:** the cross-session OBJECTIVITY property is intact — different sessions computing the same pair's spread arrive at the same percentile result regardless of weight-level narrative bias, because the computation is classical-method-delegated rather than Claude-judgment-based, and the resulting number is still measured and recorded at entry (`percentile_at_entry`, Section 6). **What does NOT survive** is its standing as a 2.24 MITIGATION: an objective number that excludes no candidate cannot be credited with filtering out weight-level-convergent false positives before entry. Component (a)'s standing is reduced from a filter to a record. The divergence's *existence* is therefore still grounded in a quantitative fact — but as an observation taken alongside the entry, not as a condition of it.]** *Residual:* narrative-quality assessment remains weight-level-bias-exposed; partial — **[rev 15 — and the residual RISES: with the anchor no longer excluding anything, the divergence-existence layer contributes no filtering, so 2.24's exposure at entry now rests on the adversarial counter-argument alone.]** See Constraint 3.

- **2.26 (RL-post-training-induced decision-token overconfidence, rev 2 added per cycle 1 T1.5):** reinforces 2.13 mechanism on the expected-return input to entry criterion 5's financing gate. *Mitigation:* **[rev 11 correction, owner directive 2026-08-18 — none; the dual-anchor gate cited here never existed outside this pre-mortem's prose and is removed, see entry criterion 5 / Constraint 4.]** *Residual:* same as 2.13, now structural and unmitigated. See Constraint 4.

- **2.28 (memory-mediated cross-session contamination, rev 6 added per cycle 5 W8/screen-ii):** *distinct from 2.24 (run-to-run weight-level variance) and from 2.10 (single-session injection):* a prior session's persisted artifacts steer a later thesis-construction session's behaviour — a newly-added `AI_Trading_Foundation.md` disadvantage, not a restatement of 2.24. E's own Section 6 logging architecture (`Decision_Log.md` per-pair fields: `pair_id`, `spread_definition`, `reference_window`, `percentile_at_entry`, `reconvergence_thresholds`, `short_stop_pct`, `short_stop_rationale` — **[rev 11 field-list sync, owner directive 2026-08-18: `anchor_2_reference_set_size` dropped with Anchor 2's removal, see entry criterion 5; `short_stop_pct`/`short_stop_rationale` added per Rev 45, see the eligibility rule]**) is durable and session-external, and the pair-prioritization rule already establishes that later thesis-construction sessions operate across the full candidate pool and accumulated strategy state — a plausible vector for exactly this channel. *Mitigation:* the repo-wide rule that operational free text (decision-log prose, alerts, run-log notes) is a report, not an instruction, is E's compensating control here — the same control `AI_Trading_Foundation.md` cites for this item generally. *Residual:* this is a detection gap, not a closed channel — Constraint 3 / KL #16's existing threshold-clustering review trigger has only one causal category (weight-level bias) for observed `reconvergence_thresholds` clustering across pairs; a later session anchoring on a prior session's persisted threshold choice would produce the identical observable signature and be misattributed to 2.24 alone. Distinguishing the two channels mechanistically is out of this revision's scope (see RESIDUAL); flagged here so the top-level omission is not repeated. *Confluence-list classification:* see the Section 5/preamble cross-walk below — not in confluence list, since its mitigation is not sizing-related.

**Binding core-edge constraints (req 7 confrontation, rev 2 architecture added per cycle 1 T1.4).**

E's core edge is AI narrative synthesis identifying within-industry-group narrative divergence between two specific names. Four distinct constraints materially affect this edge.

*Cross-constraint flowing limitation (rev 19 current; rev 7 history retained).* The pair's six-month exit and financing gates remain, but neither the retired 2%/4% rule nor the retired 10%/75% envelopes supplies a current magnitude bound. The AI-chosen CaR budget has no numeric ceiling and is exposed to the 2.4/2.13/2.15/2.18/2.19/2.24/2.26 confluence; the seven-factor record is procedural only. **[rev 8 correction, cycle 8 W3]** This paragraph previously also named a "mandatory size attack" here; struck for the same reason as the eligibility rule above — entry criterion 2 is narrative-only and no distinct size-specific adversarial mechanism is defined anywhere in this section; see Known Limitation 18. *Historical rev7 correction:* during the 2026-07-28→2026-08-05 envelope era, E's two independently capped names made the correct per-pair figure ~20%, rather than the single-name 10% stated by rev6; that correction is preserved as historical arithmetic, not a live cap.

*Section 5 / preamble cross-walk (rev 19 current; rev 7 history retained):* 2.4, 2.13, 2.15, 2.18, 2.19, 2.24, and 2.26 all retain unbounded sizing exposure subject only to the procedural budget controls. The prior envelope-based magnitude mitigation is retired. The historical rev6/rev7 envelope derivation—including its pair-specific 20% correction—does not describe a current control. Other disadvantages retain their separately stated mitigations or residuals; no envelope is credited as current compensation.

*Constraint 1 — 2.6 no access to private information.* E's narrative-divergence theses about why L is under-narrated relative to S are exactly where channel checks, expert calls, and management access provide the professional-investor edge that E structurally lacks. Sell-side reports (typical input to thesis construction) often incorporate analyst channel-check intelligence — providing indirect 2.6 access through analyst laundering. *Flowing limitation (rev 2 added):* (i) entry criterion 6's public-information compliance check — any private-information-class phrase in reasoning invalidates the thesis at construction; (ii) sell-side handling clause per Strategy C rev 19 precedent — theses where sell-side's private-information-derived conclusions are load-bearing are inadmissible; thesis must be reconstructible from primary public sources without sell-side's private-information-derived conclusions as load-bearing rationale. *Universe limitation flowing:* theses requiring private-information edge sources are excluded by construction. *Residual:* (i) sell-side may inadvertently launder private-information-derived conclusions in ways Claude cannot fully audit; (ii) public-only thesis space is structurally narrower than the full edge-domain.

*Constraint 2 — 2.4 narrative over-fit on pair narratives.* Self-reference closure parallel to Strategy A pre-mortem rev 7 Constraint 2, B Constraint 1, C Constraint 2, D Constraint 1. *Flowing limitation:* (i) entry criterion 2 (adversarial counter-argument) as graded discriminator (~30% empirical reduction **[rev 14, 2026-08-25 — UNSOURCED; see the rev-12 provenance flag at Section 5's 2.4 entry. AR_orc `premortem-E-2026-a3` cycle 11 T1-1 sustained this figure across all its recurrences and rev 12 flagged only the Section 5 locus. This locus matters most of the four: after rev 13 dropped the divergence anchor's gating force, flowing limitation (i) is the SOLE at-entry flowing limitation on 2.4, so an unsourced magnitude is now the only number quantifying what stands between a narrative-only pair and a live entry. Strategy C's companion section carries the same flag at its own Constraint 2 (rev 15 T1-4); E's did not until now.]**); ~~(ii) rev 2 entry criterion 3 quantitative-divergence-anchor (95th-percentile pair-spread test, rev 3 tightened from 80th) as external-to-Claude-reasoning verification of divergence magnitude.~~ **[rev 13, owner directive 2026-08-25 — the anchor's gating force is DROPPED (see entry criterion 3); it survives only as a measured, recorded observation (Section 6 `percentile_at_entry`), not as verification reachable before entry. Flowing limitation (ii) no longer applies — the graded adversarial counter-argument at (i) is now the sole at-entry flowing limitation on 2.4.]** ~~*Universe limitation flowing:* pairs whose thesis cannot be grounded in a quantitatively-extreme spread are excluded.~~ **[rev 13 — STRUCK. The universe genuinely widens: with the anchor no longer gating admission, a pair whose spread is not quantitatively extreme is no longer excluded from E's operating universe on that basis. Any pair meeting entry criterion 3's correlation test and the strategy's other criteria is now admissible regardless of its measured percentile — a real widening of the accepted universe, stated as such.]** ~~*Residual:* ~70% structural after counter-argument; the quantitative anchor verifies divergence magnitude exists but doesn't verify the narrative's predictive validity (Claude's mapping from "narrative divergence exists" to "L will outperform S over the next 6 months" is still weight-level-bias-exposed).~~ **[rev 13 — *Residual, risen:* with both the at-entry verification and the universe restriction gone, 2.4's residual RISES correspondingly. E now admits a wider set of narrative-only pairs with only the graded adversarial counter-argument (i) standing between a seductive-but-unverified narrative and a live entry. This is a real increase in accepted risk, stated plainly — not a wording change.]**

*Constraint 3 — 2.24 cross-session inconsistency / adversarial-review compromise.* *Load-bearing for E* because adversarial review across multiple sessions is the primary thesis-quality discipline (no other independent edge filter exists in E's mechanism). 2.24's same-weight-level-bias-cuts-across-sessions property means apparent agreement across sessions is weight-level convergence, not genuine independence. *Flowing limitation with mitigation surface + verification surface (rev 3 expanded per cycle 2 T2.C; rev 4 component (b) reframed per cycle 3 T2.F):*
   - *Component (a) — divergence-existence layer 2.24 mitigation:* ~~rev 2 entry criterion 3 quantitative-divergence-anchor (95th-percentile pair-spread test, rev 3 tightened from 80th) as external-to-reasoning verification.~~ **[rev 13, owner directive 2026-08-25 — restated: the anchor no longer gates entry (see entry criterion 3); the pair's spread percentile is still measured and recorded at entry (Section 6 `percentile_at_entry`).]** The spread fact is not Claude-produced; it's a classical-method-computed observation against historical distribution. ~~*This is the genuine 2.24 mitigation* — different sessions evaluating the same spread arrive at the same percentile result regardless of weight-level narrative bias.~~ **[rev 13 — precise about what survives and what does not: the cross-session-OBJECTIVITY property SURVIVES unchanged — different sessions computing the same pair's spread still arrive at the same percentile result regardless of weight-level narrative bias, because the computation is classical-method-delegated, not Claude-judgment-based. What does NOT survive is its force as a 2.24 MITIGATION: with no admission gate behind it, the percentile no longer EXCLUDES any candidate pair, so it can no longer be credited with filtering out weight-level-convergent false positives before entry. Component (a)'s standing is reduced from a filter to a record — an objective number is still logged, but nothing is blocked by it.]**
   - *Component (b) — narrative-validity post-hoc verification surface (rev 4 reframed):* entry criterion 1's quantitative-reconvergence-threshold requirement reduces the narrative's predictive content to a falsifiable quantitative claim that is session-stable (the threshold is a written number) and externally-verifiable post-hoc (the public event either does or does not meet the threshold). *This is NOT a 2.24 mitigation at the threshold-choice layer* — different sessions could converge on similarly-biased threshold choices due to weight-level bias on what value to commit to. **[rev 6, 2026-08-03, cycle 5 W8 — dual-cause reframing: or converge because a later session's threshold choice was anchored on a prior session's persisted `reconvergence_thresholds` value in `Decision_Log.md` rather than produced independently — memory-mediated cross-session contamination (2.28), distinct from 2.24 (2.24 is run-to-run weight-level *variance*; 2.28 is a prior session's persisted artifact steering a later session's behaviour, even when no rule is violated in doing so). The two causes produce an identical observable signature (clustered threshold values across pairs) and cannot be told apart from the clustering alone — see KL #16.]** Rev 3's "dual-component flowing limitation" framing oversold component (b) as a 2.24 mitigation; rev 4 reframes honestly: component (b) provides post-hoc verifiability (the threshold either fires or doesn't, externally checkable) which aids ex-post edge-decay detection but does not eliminate ex-ante 2.24 exposure on threshold choice. The reduction to falsifiable form is operationally valuable for KL #8 review trigger but is not a 2.24 binding-constraint mitigation.
   
   Cycle 2 T2.C surfaced that rev 2's flowing limitation operated only at the divergence-existence layer (component (a)) while load-bearing 2.24 exposure includes the narrative-validity layer; rev 3 added component (b) as an attempted second mitigation; cycle 3 T2.F surfaced that component (b) doesn't actually mitigate 2.24 at the threshold-choice layer; rev 4 reframes component (b) honestly as post-hoc verification surface. *Universe limitation flowing:* ~~(i) pairs without a quantitatively-extreme spread relative to historical distribution are excluded;~~ **[rev 15, 2026-08-30, per AR_orc `premortem-E-2026-a3` cycle 12 Tier 1 (i) — STRUCK, mirroring the fix rev 13 already made at Constraint 2's own universe-limitation clause one paragraph away and left undone here. With entry criterion 3's ≥ 95th-percentile requirement no longer gating admission (rev 13, owner directive), a pair whose spread is not quantitatively extreme is **no longer excluded** from E's operating universe on that basis. Any pair meeting criterion 3's correlation test and the strategy's other criteria is admissible regardless of its measured percentile. The universe genuinely widens; clause (i) as written asserted an exclusion that no longer happens. Clause (ii) below is UNAFFECTED — the quantitative-reconvergence-threshold requirement is entry criterion 1's mechanism, not the spread anchor, and it still excludes theses that will not commit to falsifiable predictions.]** (ii) theses without quantitative reconvergence thresholds are excluded — narratives must commit to specific falsifiable predictions. *Residual:* (i) Claude's identification of which trailing-252-day distribution to use for the percentile test is now mechanism-enforced via the share-price-ratio rule per criterion 3 (rev 3); (ii) weight-level bias on the narrative's *choice of which quantitative threshold to commit to* remains exposed (rev 4 honestly framed) — different sessions could converge on similarly-biased thresholds. The reduction to falsifiable form provides post-hoc verification value but is not a 2.24 mitigation at threshold choice; *the load-bearing 2.24 exposure at this layer is therefore an accepted architectural residual* (KL #16 added). **[rev 6, 2026-08-03, cycle 5 W8 — dual-cause note: this residual is properly 2.24-or-2.28 exposure — memory-mediated anchoring on a prior session's persisted threshold (2.28) produces the same observable clustering signature as weight-level bias (2.24) and is not distinguishable from it by the clustering alone; see KL #16, rev 6.]**

*Constraint 4 — 2.13/2.26 jointly: miscalibration on expected-return input to financing gate.* The entry gate at criterion 5 (financing cost ≤ 15% of expected return) load-bears on a directional magnitude estimate produced by an AI with hard-wired persistent overestimation of returns. **[rev 11 correction, owner directive 2026-08-18 — this Constraint previously described a "dual-anchor gate" (rev 3, cycle 2 T2.A) as the flowing limitation: financing cost had to also clear 15% of a trailing-252-day historical-median pair-mean-reversion magnitude across a deterministic ≥10-pair reference set, cross-checking Claude's own expected-return estimate against a data-driven baseline. That second anchor existed only in this pre-mortem's prose — never in `Strategy.md`'s entry criteria, never coded in `financing_cost_ok()` — and is removed by owner directive 2026-08-18 (see entry criterion 5). Its removal does not reduce any control the code ever actually enforced, since `financing_cost_ok()` has only ever computed the single test below; it removes a check this pre-mortem claimed was independently cross-verifying Claude's estimate.]** *Flowing limitation: none.* The single surviving test (criterion 5) checks financing cost against Claude's own articulated expected return with no independent, data-driven cross-check — the graded adversarial counter-argument (entry criterion 2, Constraint 2) and the mandatory adversarial attack on size are the only disciplines bearing on this input, and neither is specific to the expected-return *magnitude* the financing gate divides by. *Residual, stated plainly:* 2.13/2.26 miscalibration on the financing gate's numerator is now **structural and unmitigated** — a size that clears the gate does so because Claude's own estimate says it should, with nothing external to check that estimate against. This is a genuine widening of the residual as previously *documented* (rev 3–10's text), not a new risk introduced today: it restores the residual to what `financing_cost_ok()` has always actually delivered in code. Review trigger: portfolio-level financing drag > 25% (Section 4 indicator) remains the only detection surface, now doing the whole job post-hoc rather than backstopping an entry-time check. KL #15 (below) records Anchor 2's history and removal rather than a non-stationarity residual, which is now moot.

#### 6. Dated failure indicators for monthly review

E's pair holdings are 1-6 month theses; monthly review checks position state and edge-decay metrics on closed positions.

- [Each monthly review] **Open-pair check.** Per open pair: any news, management event, regulatory development, M&A announcement, or competitive move in the prior month affecting either L or S → close if thesis relationship broken or invalidation criteria fired.
- [Each monthly review] **Public-information compliance recheck.** Per open pair: does the thesis still rely only on public information? If at any point thesis leaks into non-public territory (e.g., a sell-side report driving conviction is later identified as private-information-derived), close.
- [Each monthly review] **Borrow-rate operational check.** Per active short leg: current short borrow rate. Close if any pair's borrow > 10% annualized (operational risk management — distinct from edge-decay financing-trend at Section 4).
- [Each monthly review] **Correlation-breakdown monitoring.** Per open pair: 60-day rolling correlation. If < 0.3 → pair-relationship-broken exit per criterion 5.
- [Each monthly review, rev 2 added per cycle 1 T1.10] **Portfolio-level financing drag computation.** Trailing-12-month sum of borrow costs / trailing-12-month gross pair P&L (across closed positions in that window). > 25% → flag per Section 4 indicator.
- [Each monthly review, rev 2 added per cycle 1 T1.10] **Mean entry-time borrow cost trend.** 6-month moving average of entry-time borrow costs. Trend rising > 50% in 12 months → flag per Section 4 indicator.
- [Each monthly review, rev 2 added per cycle 1 T1.12] **Entry-percentile-vs-realized-P&L rank correlation.** Computed by classical-method delegation on closed positions (rolling 10 closes minimum). Rank correlation between at-entry quantitative-divergence-anchor percentile and realized P&L < 0 → flag per Section 4.
- [Each monthly review, rev 10 added per AR_orc `premortem-E-2026-a3` cycle 9 T1-2] **Size-attack transmission audit (Known Limitation 18's review trigger, made operative here).** **Owner: M4 (Monthly Action Conversion)** — named deliberately, and after checking rather than assuming. M4 is the only monthly-cadence routine whose declared read scope includes `strategy/08_pre_mortems.md` (the slice-map row `W3 / W4 / M4 / Q4 / A3 / D1 / AR_orchestrator … + 08_pre_mortems.md for reviews`), and it may query all of `events.decision_log`, so it is the one monthly routine that can actually reach this checklist and evaluate it. **M2 is disqualified twice over** — the slice map gives M2 only `07_strategy_e.md` + `01`, so a bullet addressed to M2 would be unreachable; and M2's own body excludes E's open book ("Exclude pairs where either leg is in the open E book"), so it does not look at entered pairs at all. A trigger addressed to a routine that never reads it is exactly the failure mode cycle 9's T1-2 identified, and rev 10 is not going to reproduce it one line lower. **The check:** for every E pair entered since the previous monthly review, read that thesis's `events.decision_log` entry and confirm the adversarial counter-argument on record **attacked the size** — that it stated what the pair would cost if the thesis is wrong and argued whether that loss is acceptable, per the `Experiment_Parameters.md` rev 18 text reproduced at KL18. Record the number of entries checked and the number carrying a size attack. **Consequence for a specific flagged pair:** a pair entered with no recorded size attack is logged by name in M4's own `events.decision_log` cycle entry as a KL18 transmission miss, and the omission is repaired by recording the size attack against that pair's live position at the current mark before the next monthly review — a retrospective attack is worth less than a pre-entry one, but an unattacked live position with no record at all is the condition KL18 exists to prevent. **Escalation, with its owner:** any miss escalates the in-section amendment of E's entry criterion 2 from a deferred machinery question to an active one, raised by M4 as `ops.sp_raise_alert('warning','M4','premortem_live_gate_defect', …)` — an already-registered `ops.alert_policy` category for exactly this class (a documented control that does not reach the executed path), warning rather than critical because an open critical enters `bigquery/107`'s blocking-criticals term and would halt order staging — so it reaches the operator by email rather than resting in a routine's unmonitored chat. A review at which no new pairs were entered is recorded as such and is **not** a miss. **[rev 14 DISCLOSURE, 2026-08-25, per AR_orc `premortem-E-2026-a3` cycle 11 T1-3 — WHAT THIS CHECK CANNOT DO, stated at the locus where it is executed rather than only in a revision note. This audit is a **presence test, not a correctness test.** It confirms that the record contains a stated cost and an acceptability argument; it does **not** and cannot evaluate whether the acceptability judgment was graded correctly, so it passes a self-servingly-graded "acceptable" exactly as it passes a correctly-graded one. That is not a hypothetical failure mode for this strategy: the biases this same document catalogs at 2.13 and 2.26 (persistent overestimation) and 2.24 (weight-level convergence across sessions) are specifically biases toward a self-serving grade, and with both CaR envelopes retired (Rev 19) this graded, self-audited check is the substantive control on E's now-unceilinged per-thesis sizing. **Why the gap is disclosed and not closed here:** closing it means authoring a grading standard against which a size attack's verdict could be judged — a new control, which is machinery and outside SL2's documentary route (B), the same boundary this limitation's transmission residual already sits behind. It stays escalated on that basis, and the escalation path below is unchanged. **What the check IS still worth, so this disclosure is not read as retiring it:** a pair entered with no size attack at all is caught by it, and that — not miscalibration — is the failure mode KL18 was authored against.]** This item defines no new indicator, moves no threshold, and gates no entry.
- [At 10 pair closes] **Preliminary edge assessment.** Convergence rate, convergence-vs-timeline ratio, cumulative excess real return vs SGOV. All thresholds per Section 4.
- [At 15 pair closes = 30 trades] **Gate evaluation per Experiment_Parameters.md.** **[rev 10 added per AR_orc `premortem-E-2026-a3` cycle 9 T1-2 — the cumulative leg of KL18's review trigger, which rev 9 stated in prose ("Also audit at the 15-pair gate") without ever itemizing it here.]** The gate evaluation additionally includes a **cumulative size-attack transmission audit** across every pair entered to date: the count of entries carrying a recorded size attack against the total, and every named exception. **Owner: M4**, as above. A cumulative miss rate that is anything other than zero is reported in the gate evaluation as a live control-transmission finding, not as a documentation footnote — the whole point of KL18 is that a control which governs on paper is worth nothing if it never reaches the desk.

*Logging additions required for Section 6 indicator implementation (rev 3 added per cycle 2 T2.D):* The entry-percentile-vs-realized-P&L rank correlation indicator (Section 4) and the per-pair monthly review require specific data fields not present in current Portfolio_Ledger.md or Decision_Log.md schema. The required additions:

**At thesis formation, Decision_Log.md per-pair entry must include:**
- `pair_id` — concatenation in format `LtickerSticker` (e.g., `KOPEP` for long Coca-Cola / short PepsiCo)
- `spread_definition` — `price_ratio` | `log_spread` (mechanism-enforced choice per the 3x share-price-ratio rule) **[rev 13, owner directive 2026-08-25 — reworded off "per criterion 3" gate language now that criterion 3 no longer gates on the spread; the field is a recorded measurement, not a gate output. The underlying mechanism-enforced choice rule (KL #14) is unchanged.]**
- `reference_window` — `252` (trading days; default unless empirically calibrated otherwise at 15-pair gate) **[rev 13 — this is the measurement window for the recorded spread observation, independent of admission; unchanged by the anchor's removal.]**
- `percentile_at_entry` — computed value **0.00-1.00** **[rev 13, owner directive 2026-08-25 — corrected from "0.95-1.00 per criterion 3": entry criterion 3's percentile gate is DROPPED, so entries are no longer restricted to the top 5%; the field now spans its true full range. This correction is mandatory — leaving 0.95-1.00 would make this logging spec contradict reality on the first sub-95th-percentile entry.]**
- ~~`anchor_2_reference_set_size` — count of qualifying pairs in Anchor 2 set (≥ 10 per criterion 5)~~ **[rev 11, owner directive 2026-08-18 — Anchor 2 removed (entry criterion 5); this field logged a gate that never existed in `Strategy.md` or `financing_cost_ok()`. Dropped, not replaced.]**
- `reconvergence_thresholds` — the specific quantitative thresholds per criterion 1 (text or JSON-structured threshold list, immutable)
- `short_stop_pct`, `short_stop_rationale` — **[rev 11 addition, owner directive 2026-08-18]** the pair's mandatory short-leg stop distance and its justification, set before sizing and frozen for the position's life (Rev 45; see the eligibility rule) — `Strategy.md`'s own field requirement, reproduced here for Section 6 completeness now that the underlying stop is real

**At pair close, Portfolio_Ledger.md per-pair close must include:**
- `pair_id` — matching the entry record
- `realized_pnl_dollars` — net P&L on the pair, after commissions and accumulated short-financing costs
- `realized_pnl_percent` — as fraction of pair gross capital deployed ~~(4% per pair)~~ **[Rev 43, 2026-07-28 — superseded: pair gross capital deployed is now the pair's AI-chosen CaR (sum of both legs, sized to the hedge ratio; see the eligibility rule in Section 1), not a fixed 4% figure — the field's denominator is per-pair-actual, read from the thesis's decision-log entry, not a constant]**
- `holding_days` — calendar days from entry to close
- `exit_reason` — `convergence` | `invalidation` | `timeline` | `correlation_breakdown` | `short_leg_stop` (one of five per Section 1 exit rules) **[rev 12 addition, 2026-08-24 — `short_leg_stop` added, closing the logging lockout Rev 45's mandatory short-leg stop (2026-08-18) created (AR_orc `premortem-E-2026-a3` cycle 11, item T1-2): a stop-triggered exit previously had no legal value to log. See Section 1 Exit rules above.]**

**The entry-percentile-vs-realized-P&L rank correlation indicator** computes Spearman rank correlation between `percentile_at_entry` and `realized_pnl_percent` across the trailing 10 closed pairs at each monthly review. Indicator is uncomputable (and therefore not flagged) until 10 pairs have closed; this is an accepted property given E's declared frequency of 6-12 pairs/year (10 pairs ≈ 12-20 months active operation). **[rev 13, owner directive 2026-08-25 — this indicator SURVIVES the anchor's removal and in fact IMPROVES. With entry criterion 3's percentile gate dropped, `percentile_at_entry` will finally vary across its full 0.00-1.00 range at future entries instead of being clamped to 0.95-1.00, so the Spearman rank correlation computed here can actually test whether entry percentile predicts realized P&L — a test it was structurally incapable of performing while every entry sat in the top 5% (a rank correlation computed over a near-constant input carries no information). This is a genuine analytic gain from the anchor's removal, recorded as one.]**

Corrected statement: the divergence percentile is still COMPUTED and RECORDED at entry as `percentile_at_entry`, but it verifies nothing at admission, so nothing now stands between narrative divergence and a live entry except the graded adversarial counter-argument at Constraint 2 flowing limitation (i).
